Model before rewriting:

find x: int(-2..0)
find y: int(2..3)
find z: int(-10..-1)

such that

(x ** y) = z

   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
z
new variables:
  find z#sign_magnitude_1: bool
  find z#sign_magnitude_2: bool
  find z#sign_magnitude_3: bool
  find z#sign_magnitude_4: bool
  find z#sign_magnitude_5: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(z#sign_magnitude_4),and([!(z#sign_magnitude_3),or([!(z#sign_magnitude_2),and([!(z#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)])
  (z#sign_magnitude_5) -> (or([!(z#sign_magnitude_4),and([!(z#sign_magnitude_3),or([!(z#sign_magnitude_2),and([!(z#sign_magnitude_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]);int(1..)]))
  z#sign_magnitude_5
  (z#sign_magnitude_5) -> (or([z#sign_magnitude_1,z#sign_magnitude_2,z#sign_magnitude_3,z#sign_magnitude_4;int(1..)]))
  or([and([SATInt(SignMagnitude, [z#sign_magnitude_1,z#sign_magnitude_2,z#sign_magnitude_3,z#sign_magnitude_4,z#sign_magnitude_5;int(1..)] [-10, -1]) >= -10,SATInt(SignMagnitude, [z#sign_magnitude_1,z#sign_magnitude_2,z#sign_magnitude_3,z#sign_magnitude_4,z#sign_magnitude_5;int(1..)] [-10, -1]) <= -1;int(1..)]);int(1..)])
